Ash: Make the scheduled reboot dialog own its widget

This changelist is part of an ongoing series to move Ash widgets off
the deprecated NATIVE_WIDGET_OWNS_WIDGET and WIDGET_OWNS_NATIVE_WIDGET
ownership modes and onto CLIENT_OWNS_WIDGET, the mode views recommends
for all widgets. With it, the code that creates a widget owns it and
decides when it is destroyed. This removes a common source of dangling
pointers and window teardown bugs.

The scheduled reboot dialog now owns its widget instead of tracking it
through a raw pointer, and destroys it when the user closes the dialog
or the reboot scheduler dismisses or replaces it.

Bug: 338254375
Test: CQ

Roll Amd64 AFDO from 160.0.8156.0_pre1741658_rc-r1-merged to 160.0.8160.0_pre1744043_rc-r1-merged

This CL may cause a small binary size increase, roughly proportional
to how long it's been since our last AFDO profile roll. For larger
increases (around or exceeding 100KB), please file go/crostc-bug.

Please note that, despite rolling to chrome/android, this profile is
used for both Linux and Android.

If this roll has caused a breakage, revert this CL and set the roller
to dry run mode using the controls here:
https://autoroll.skia.org/r/afdo-chromium

45edfd5ceadd8cb85e8c864be06805c9e7ec4605
parent
5d55b5eac7c6e032d6792b7244e38a583ade60e7 [ diff ] 
[CRD Linux] Set up the configured host type in start-host

start-host now sets up the configured host type, so re-running it
repairs the setup. It never changes the configured host type. That is
left to migrate-host, so that switching between any host types has one
code path.

- The target is --host-type, else the configured host type, else the
  default.
- If --host-type differs from the configured host type, start-host
  fails and points to migrate-host, even after the host was deleted.
- For a multi-process target, it re-runs with sudo when not root,
  passing --user-name, and checks the local user as root.
- The single-process host is set up without sudo, unless start-host is
  run as root. HostType::NeedsStartHostHook() is removed.
- The Linux setup steps move out of start_host_main.cc into focused
  helpers in start_host_linux.cc (renamed from start_host_as_root.cc).

[Copy Trigger] Keep profile-level copies in their profile
📋 Metadata

9de620b21368a61b427cf2ab8d42643eaaeafe48
[ log ]
author
Elias Homsi <eliashomsi@google.com>
Sat Nov 14 05:46:02 2026
committer

Sat Nov 14 05:46:02 2026
tree
8cbefc7733cfaf1a648aebd9be906e866f667202
parent
2a56bf686040e5f6c305f06e43a48a593557bea3 [ diff ] 
[Copy Trigger] Keep profile-level copies in their profile

A copy kept in managed Chrome by the OnDataCopiedEnterpriseConnector
policy is restored for pastes into any profile of the browser, including
unmanaged, off-the-record and other-organization ones, as long as the
tab it was copied from is still open. When the policy that kept the copy
only applies to the profile it was made from, those other profiles are
outside of what the organization manages, so the data can leave managed
Chrome through a second profile.

Record whether the scan that kept a copy ran for a profile-level policy
and, if so, only restore the copy for pastes into the profile it was
copied from. Other profiles get the "kept in managed Chrome" message the
OS clipboard holds, like an outside application would. Copies kept by a
browser-level (CBCM) policy are still restored in every profile since
the whole browser is managed then, and so are copies kept by Data
Controls rules, which can already target other profiles explicitly.

Hold pastes into the find bar to the same restrictions. It restored
kept, blocked and still-scanning copies from any profile, from where
they could be copied back out without going through the copy checks.

Fixed: 585138668
Bug: 325455508

Remove dead WebViewTest.SelectShowHide entry from arm64 Wayland filter

chrome/browser/apps/guest_view/web_view_browsertest.cc is only compiled
into browser_tests when is_chromeos && enable_extensions
(chrome/browser/apps/BUILD.gn), so -WebViewTest.SelectShowHide/* matches
no test in the Linux browser_tests binary. A 20-repeat UTR run of
linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi in chromium:try with a positive
--gtest_filter and --gtest_also_run_disabled_tests matched 0 tests.
Removing the dead entry does not change which tests run.

Evidence:
- WebViewTest.SelectShowHide/* (browser_tests):
  linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi 0 matching tests (x64 30d: 0/0, arm64
  unfiltered 30d: 0/0).

Remove redundant DisabledWhenOccluded from ozone-linux mutter filter

Since https://crrev.com/c/8724387,
FedCmAccountSelectionViewBrowserTest.DisabledWhenOccluded checks
supports_global_screen_coordinates and calls GTEST_SKIP() on platforms
without global screen coordinates (all Ozone/Wayland compositors,
including weston where the test already runs unfiltered). That makes
the mutter-only filter entry and its stale crbug.com/40688401 comment
redundant.

The ozone-linux.browser_tests_mutter filter is shared: besides
linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi it applies to the x64
linux-wayland-mutter-rel-tests builder (CI, owned by
chrome-linux-engprod@google.com) and its CQ mirror
linux-wayland-mutter-rel, so this change affects those builders too;
the 20-repeat UTR probes covered both.

Evidence:
- FedCmAccountSelectionViewBrowserTest.DisabledWhenOccluded
  (browser_tests_mutter): linux-arm64-wayland-rel-fyi 20/20 expected
  SKIP (SKIPPED_BY_TEST_BODY, 0 failures),
  linux-wayland-mutter-rel-tests 20/20 expected SKIP
  (SKIPPED_BY_TEST_BODY, 0 failures), x64 30d: 5/2644, arm64

039d5db1e78c3937d5c8e35d2254ca4a1eea541e [ diff ] 
[Media Perf] Clean stale glances processes, CSVs, and column parsing

Windows GLANCES_KILL_CMD used $_ pipeline variables inside a double-
quoted powershell -Command string, which expands to empty when sshd uses
PowerShell and leaves python -m glances running across tasks. Over time,
hundreds of leaked glances processes lock C:/Users/Public/glances_*.csv,
grow the CSVs to multi-GB sizes, and starve Chrome startup until
ChromeDriver times out waiting for DevToolsActivePort.

Kill glances and python processes via Stop-Process and taskkill without
$_ variables, purge stale glances CSVs, trace files, and scoped_dir temp
profiles during terminate_chromedriver(), delete any leftover CSV before
starting monitoring, and accept underscored cpu_total headers while
skipping percentage battery readings for power_consumption_watts.

tree
e1e9280327c466c18756de6fb74f3ea33cfb09e2
parent
7031337d1963d63770d94db582071499d430fda5 [ diff ] 
WebUI: Cancel cr-ripple animations when removing ripples

This CL cancels active Web Animations on `.ripple` elements in
`<cr-ripple>` whenever a ripple is removed or `<cr-ripple>` disconnects
from the DOM.

Previously, `showRipple_()` started an 800ms+ Web Animation with
`fill: 'forwards'` on `.ripple`, and `hideRipple_()` removed `.ripple`
after a 150ms `opacity` fade-out without calling `cancel()` on either
`Animation`. In Blink, removing an `Element` from the DOM does not
cancel its active `KeyframeEffect`: detaching `.ripple` aborts its
compositor animation and leaves the `fill: 'forwards'` animation
ticking on `document.timeline` at 60Hz for the remaining ~600ms (~36
main-thread frames per click), keeping a `MainThreadAnimation`
`FrameSequenceTrackerV3` sequence open across the interaction window.

In a GPU-composited Linux Release trace across 6 toolbar button clicks
(4.5s window in `chrome://webui-toolbar.top-chrome`), canceling
detached `<cr-ripple>` animations eliminates all post-removal 60Hz
main-thread animation ticks and orphaned `MainThreadAnimation`
sequences, reducing `ProxyMain::BeginMainFrame` frames and
`Blink.Paint.UpdateTime` passes from 226 to 58 (-74.3%) and
`BeginMainFrame` time from 185.1 ms to 85.0 ms (-54.1%).

[Android] Remove --disable-default-browser-promo and its expired flag

The clank-default-browser-promo-role-manager chrome://flags entry was
added in crrev.com/c/5909707 only to make testing the then-new Role
Manager default browser promo easier. Its expiry milestone is M140, so
at M160 it is unconditionally expired: it is hidden from chrome://flags
and any stored value is dropped before flags are converted to switches.

The entry was the only in-tree way to set
--disable-default-browser-promo, which crrev.com/c/2277042 added in 2020
as a developer command line argument. Apart from the unit test removed
here, nothing in the tree passes it, so remove the switch and the
DefaultBrowserPromoUtils check that read it as well. After this, the
Role Manager promo can no longer be suppressed from the command line.

The LoginCustomFlags enum value is kept, since enums.xml entries are
never removed.

parent
d5b009aa29fcc7d21bdcb1dd0f377a8fdbb9e9c8 [ diff ] 
[Cast Remoting] Decouple MediaRemoter from mirroring concepts

MediaRemoter is the Mirroring Service's end of the media::mojom::Remoter
and RemotingSource interfaces. It does not own the streaming session.
OpenscreenSessionHost does, and decides what to stream when remoting is
not active. The MediaRemoter API still talked about mirroring, and
events that started in the session host called back into the session
host through MediaRemoter::Client.

Split the API by direction, like the mojom interfaces:
- MediaRemoter::Client takes commands from the source: StartRemoting()
  and StopRemoting(reason). These replace RequestRemotingStreaming()
  and RestartMirroringStreaming().
- MediaRemoter takes events from its client: OnRemotingStarted(),
  OnRemotingStartFailed(), OnRemotingStopped(reason),
  OnReadyForRemoting() and OnSourceChanged(). These replace
  OnRemotingFailed(), OnMirroringResumed() and the public Stop().

An event from the client no longer causes a call back into the client.
OpenscreenSessionHost now leaves remoting directly, and destroying the
remoter no longer calls the client.

RedirectionSessionHost, the other MediaRemoter::Client, shows the
problem well. It has no mirroring to go back to, but it had to
implement RestartMirroringStreaming() because that was the only way to
learn that remoting was over. To end its session after a stream error,
it called MediaRemoter::Stop() and waited for the call to come back
through RestartMirroringStreaming(). It now ends its session in
StopRemoting(), and after a stream error it tells the remoter with
OnRemotingStopped() and ends the session itself.

Also rename MediaRemoter's MIRRORING state to READY, log the reason
when the remoter stops remoting, and remove the redundant
OpenscreenSessionHost::Negotiate() helper.

Bug: 379361280
Test: mirroring_unittests --gtest_filter=MediaRemoterTest.*:OpenscreenSessionHostTest.*

30b1a3b4df0762bb0fac4b51661dd95629076fb3
parent
d2bc00d8c2a9731c7813a0916289b1d0cd97b850 [ diff ] 
[Cast Remoting] Handle a remoting OFFER that is cancelled in flight

An OFFER cannot be recalled. If the source stopped remoting while the
remoting OFFER was in flight, OpenscreenSessionHost kept mirroring and
forgot about the OFFER. When the receiver then accepted it, the session
host handled the remoting ANSWER as a mirroring one and tried to create
mirroring senders from remoting configs, which reaches a NOTREACHED in
media::cast::AudioEncoder.

Remember that the pending remoting OFFER was cancelled, and wait for
the reply before telling the source that remoting is available again:
- If the receiver accepts the OFFER, it is in remoting mode, so
  negotiate mirroring again.
- If the OFFER fails, mirroring never stopped. Keep it running and do
  not report a failed start to the source.

A tab switch and Remote Playback do not wait, because neither keeps
the mirroring streams.

An ANSWER error always belongs to the OFFER in flight, so only check
PendingOfferMode to decide whether a remoting start failed.

Bug: 379361280
Test: mirroring_unittests --gtest_filter=OpenscreenSessionHostTest.*

[agents] Add ruled_code_reviewer subagent and ruled-code-review skill

Add the `ruled-code-review` skill and `ruled_code_reviewer` subagent to
evaluate Chromium code changes against directory-specific in-tree
Knowledge Base rules and review skills declared in DIR_METADATA
(`agentic_review`). The skill resolves the applicable rules and skills
locally with `read_review_capabilities.py`, ported from
`//internal/agents/skills/experimental-code-review/`.

Teach the `code-review` skill to delegate in-tree rule checks to
`ruled_code_reviewer`, mirroring how it delegates to
`security_reviewer`.

[Default Browser] Remove UserValueDefaultBrowserStrings flag

Remove the UserValueDefaultBrowserStrings feature flag and its
chrome://flags entry now that the feature is enabled by default.

Specifically:
- Remove kUserValueDefaultBrowserStrings from chrome_features,
  about_flags, flag_descriptions, and flag-metadata.json.
- Remove the WebUI feature-state query plumbing
  (requestUserValueStringsFeatureState and RouteObserverMixinLit in
  default_browser_page).
- Map defaultBrowserDefault, defaultBrowserMakeDefault, and
  defaultBrowserMakeDefaultAndPin directly to the launched
  IDS_*_THANK_YOU and IDS_*_USER_VALUE strings, and delete the
  obsolete pre-experiment strings and unused screenshot hashes.
- Remove feature-off tests and TODO(crbug.com/459593729) comments.

Revert "[webui] Enable bundled code cache generation on linux-chromeos"

This reverts commit 909f9af1322d4ff9fab8a0616d62abb80d92a2f9.

Reason for revert:
LUCI Bisection has identified this change as the culprit of a build failure. See the analysis: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/bisection/compile-analysis/b/8665191551952690657

Sample failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665191551952690657

If this is a false positive, please report it at http://b.corp.google.com/createIssue?component=1199205&description=Analysis%3A+https%3A%2F%2Fchromium-review.googlesource.com%2Fc%2Fchromium%2Fsrc%2F%2B%2F8794993&format=PLAIN&priority=P3&title=Wrongly+blamed+https%3A%2F%2Fci.chromium.org%2Fui%2Fp%2Fchromium%2Fbisection%2Fcompile-analysis%2Fb%2F8665191551952690657&type=BUG

Original change's description:
> [webui] Enable bundled code cache generation on linux-chromeos
>
> Enable build-time WebUI bundled V8 code cache generation for ChromeOS
> builds where host_toolchain == current_toolchain, such as linux-chromeos
> and the linux-chromeos-rel CQ builder.
>
> Device cross-compiles (cros:target and cros:host) stay off because the
> generator links Blink. Cross-compilation would build the generator in
> cros:v8_snapshot, where nothing builds Blink today because ChromeOS
> disables use_v8_context_snapshot.
>
> Runtime use stays gated. The feature kWebUIBundledCodeCache is disabled
> by default (content/public/common/content_features.cc). Only
> WebUIReloadButtonStudy turns it on (fieldtrial_testing_config.json, with
> chromeos added in crrev.com/c/8739342), and toolbar WebUI loads only
> under that study. On linux-chromeos, this change updates resources.pak
> contents without altering runtime behavior until the study or feature
> is turned on.
>
> Bug: 525236301, 399764218
> Change-Id: I30022f3fcb3ac37cfb61008ec6a020b28a47e60c

[Agentic Suggestions][Android] Support SUIT TypeIconBadge

In arm 2 of the agentic suggestions experiment, Clank shows a small
blue dot on the top-right corner of the history icon on the left of
agentic update suggestions. The server sends it as
SuggestTemplateInfo.type_icon_badge.

Copy the TypeIconBadge proto change from google3 and draw an 8dp dot
with a 1dp border in the suggestion background color on the top-right
corner of the icon when the server sends a DOT badge. Only the icon
takes the tint. Suggestions with a badge keep their icon instead of
fetching an image, which would drop the badge.

Sat Nov 14 03:26:50 2026
tree
6bb17052676ef1ba18f548c613839a406d5e7308
parent
34c720a6ea93934a4d426f41ee1962109c28f491 [ diff ] 
PaintTiming: Reset text aggregation across frames and text clip masks

ScopedPaintTimingDetectorBlockPaintHook tracks the active container
for text paint timing aggregation via a static pointer. When an
embedding container paints a child frame synchronously, or when an
element paints a text clip mask, this pointer could remain active
across paint boundary transitions, causing subsequent text paints to
be erroneously attributed to an ancestor container.

This CL resets the active block paint hook in FramePaintTiming upon
entering frame painting and in BoxFragmentPainter::PaintTextClipMask
before rendering text clip masks, ensuring text paint timing is
properly scoped to its respective frame and painting context.

Fixed: 574505130

ae0089f9488826ec0149983e5b5120a131134b15

004a988c23225c20b43a2e64b9e3b72d581cf50e [ diff ] 
[android] Remove obsolete ShadowAnimatedStateListDrawable

Unblocks the android_deps roll to Robolectric 4.18
(https://crrev.com/c/8818432), which failed to compile
//ui/android:ui_junit_test_support (e.g.
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665204652251830737):

  ShadowAnimatedStateListDrawable.java:16: error: method addState in
  class ShadowAnimatedStateListDrawable cannot be applied to given types;
          addState(stateSet, drawable);
          ^
    required: int[],Drawable,int
    found:    int[],Drawable

Robolectric 4.18 removed ShadowStateListDrawable#addState(int[],
Drawable) and its internal state map in favor of reading directly from
the real StateListDrawable. Since Chromium's minSdkVersion is 29+,
StateListDrawable natively exposes public getStateCount(),
getStateSet(int), and getStateDrawable(int) APIs, so
ShadowAnimatedStateListDrawable is no longer needed.

[WebAudio] Validate renderSizeHint without sampleRate

Previously renderSizeHint was only validated when sampleRate was
specified.  Add validation against the maximum supported sampleRate
if not specified, then validate again after the actual device
sample rate is retrieved.

This also required allocating the AudioListenerHandler arrays with
TryAllocate() and calling Uninitialize() on rejected contexts.

0f68f211eac4a1c1d9cc495d5333aec9ca739bc2
parent
a4b6f1bfa9644748e98a87ba4ebb4e00e5143ae9 [ diff ] 
[Cast] Cap sender in-flight duration and fix trace utilization order

OpenscreenFrameSender::GetInFlightMediaDuration adds the raw RTP span of
in-flight frames to the encoder backlog, which inflates the pre-encode
drop check after static-screen capture pauses. In addition,
VideoSender::OnEncodedVideoFrame ends the frame trace slice before
updating encoder_utilization and lossiness, logging the previous frame's
metrics on every slice.

Cap the sender in-flight duration by the unacknowledged frame count
divided by the maximum frame rate, and update encoder utilization and
lossiness before ending the VideoSender trace slice.

[Agentic Suggestions][Android] Support SUIT ActionIconType BLUE_DOT

arrow. The server sends it as BLUE_DOT in

tappable, and tapping the row opens the suggestion. It keeps its accent
color instead of the icon tint.

📝 Commit Message (First Line)
Use MTC CA certificates instead of bssl::MTCAnchor for MTC path checks
📋 Metadata
commit
dd76f4b1514585da9d6ba2edab375b17b9f1400b
[ log ]
author
Lily Chen <chlily@chromium.org>
Sat Nov 14 03:02:41 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Sat Nov 14 03:02:41 2026
tree
cc109369779a02f4be357c23a90f2c2be2ee5144
parent
13dd3f49dfe0ea85e6e53010314e28be631dea26 [ diff ] 
Use MTC CA certificates instead of bssl::MTCAnchor for MTC path checks

Treat an MTC CA like any other trust anchor wherever possible, swapping
out bssl::MTCAnchor for the MTC CA cert as a ParsedCertificate.

- A path is an MTC path if its trusted certificate has an MTC CA
  extension (mtc_ca_info()). That decides whether to check CT or the
  Signer Set MTC policy, and which Chrome Root Store data to look up.
- SystemTrustStore::IsKnownMtcAnchor() is folded into IsKnownRoot().
  TrustStoreChrome::ContainsMTCAnchor() is folded into Contains(), which
  still matches MTC CA certificates by CA ID, equivalent to the previous
  match by subject.
- The MTC helpers in SystemTrustStore and TrustStoreChrome take the MTC
  CA certificate instead of a bssl::MTCAnchor, and read the CA ID from
  its mtc_ca_info().

Chrome Root Store serial number constraints now apply to all roots, not
only MTC CAs. No traditional root has such a constraint.

Also override VerifyCertificateChainDelegate::
IsMTCCosignatureVerificationResultAcceptable(),
(which takes the MTC CA certificate, instead of the deprecated
IsCosignatureVerificationResultAcceptable, which takes a
bssl::MTCAnchor.

[iOS][apc] Treat button texts as text nodes, matching blink

Blink includes button text (including when set by value or default
Submit/Reset labels) as a separate child text node. Synthesize a
matching child TEXT node on iOS for button, submit, and reset <input>
controls:
- Share text styling, CSS transforms/masking, and collapsible-whitespace
  filtering with getAttributesForTextNode(), using a fresh
  annotatedRoles array and copying host <input> geometry in actionable
  mode.
- Fall back to default 'Submit' / 'Reset' only when value is omitted
  (not when explicitly empty value=""), and skip password-redacted
  inputs or nodes past maxDepth.

[web apps] Simulate view focus in EnterFullscreenAndVerify()

Since SuppressFullscreenFromUnfocusedView became stable, the browser
rejects fullscreen requests from views without focus. browser_tests run
in parallel and can't rely on OS-level window activation, so the
requestFullscreen() call in EnterFullscreenAndVerify() is rejected with
"not granted" whenever the window lacks focus. EvalFullscreenRequest()
then waits for a display-state change that never comes and the test
times out, e.g. https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665221623905561025.

On CQ, WebAppFrameToolbarBrowserTest_AdditionalWindowingControls.
FullscreenMaximizeAndRestoreWindowWithApi cost 167 in-shard re-run
test-hours in the 7 days ending 2026-11-11.

Mark the view as focused with SetHasFocusForTesting(), as
EnterTabFullscreenThroughWebAPI() in the same file and
https://crrev.com/c/8797552 already do.

UTR on linux-rel, browser_tests, with --gtest_repeat=10 and
--gtest_filter=WebAppFrameToolbarBrowserTest_AdditionalWindowingControls.*
Results for FullscreenMaximizeAndRestoreWindowWithApi:
- Before: 4/10 failed.
  https://ci.chromium.org/ui/inv/u-mayurishinde-2026-11-13-23-59-00-b20abe4c6fc13b90
- After: 10/10 passed.
  https://ci.chromium.org/ui/inv/u-mayurishinde-2026-11-14-00-21-00-01dbb34a0f2a7d3b

parent
84a8a2005545cd4c0777fa7863ad3316d059bdb3 [ diff ] 
[webui] Enable bundled code cache generation on linux-chromeos

Enable build-time WebUI bundled V8 code cache generation for ChromeOS
builds where host_toolchain == current_toolchain, such as linux-chromeos
and the linux-chromeos-rel CQ builder.

Device cross-compiles (cros:target and cros:host) stay off because the
generator links Blink. Cross-compilation would build the generator in
cros:v8_snapshot, where nothing builds Blink today because ChromeOS
disables use_v8_context_snapshot.

Runtime use stays gated. The feature kWebUIBundledCodeCache is disabled
by default (content/public/common/content_features.cc). Only
WebUIReloadButtonStudy turns it on (fieldtrial_testing_config.json, with
chromeos added in crrev.com/c/8739342), and toolbar WebUI loads only
under that study. On linux-chromeos, this change updates resources.pak
contents without altering runtime behavior until the study or feature
is turned on.

[Agentic Suggestions] Support SUIT ActionIconType on Desktop

58ce49869444260510c8a7cae3962dab111758bf [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Check on-demand APC requests in eligibility tests

The page eligibility browser tests now verify that eligibility and
GetRecentTabs() do not request on-demand AnnotatedPageContent
extraction, using the OnDemand.EnabledReason histogram. Automatic
on-load extraction is enabled by field trial config and populates the
extraction cache for background tabs on its own schedule, so checking
the cache failed on slow debug bots even though GetRecentTabs() never
touched the extraction service.

The tests keep full coverage of both background and foreground
ineligible tabs and pass reliably on debug bots.

f2d585bb448ca10777d9200a0f3abf38980efff4
parent
c2c4c443c53d01ebe1c1e36040ee66f1327dab8d [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Focus deferred popup when fullscreen Space activates on Mac

Opening a new browser window from a fullscreen window on macOS enters a
new fullscreen Space and focuses the initial NTP's Full WebUI omnibox,
but typing sends keys to the browser window instead of the omnibox
popup.

The NTP can focus the omnibox before macOS activates the new Space, so
NativeWidgetNSWindowBridge::OrderChildren() defers attaching and showing
the popup NSWindow. While it is hidden, OmniboxPopupPresenterBase's
IsShown() returns false and RequestFocus() bails out before calling
Activate() (which would also be skipped while unattached). Once
NSWorkspaceActiveSpaceDidChangeNotification attaches and shows the
popup, nothing re-requests popup activation.

Observe OnWidgetVisibilityOnScreenChanged() in OmniboxPopupFullPresenter
so that when the popup appears on screen, it re-synchronizes its bounds
and requests focus again if the omnibox is still focused with the popup
open. Rename RefocusAfterActivation() to RequestFocusIfOmniboxFocused(),
as it now also runs when the popup appears on screen.

Tests: NativeWidgetMacTest.ChildWidgetOnInactiveSpace,
FullWebUIOmniboxInteractiveTest.*DeferredAttach*

TAG=agy
CONV=c08843e8-0eda-4b66-a027-ec935d84054b

parent
6973290221a1fcb0660eaf0966c77a7b877c9512 [ diff ] 
[chromecast] Preserve audio config lifetime in DemuxerStreamReader

In DemuxerStreamReader::UpdateAudioConfig(), validate the new audio
decoder configuration before committing it to member state. Previously,
updating chromium_audio_config_ prior to validation could invalidate
the backing storage for existing audio sample metadata when validation
failed.

Additionally, in DemuxerStreamReader::WaitForKey(), retain a copy of the
audio extra data buffer in the pending DRM key closure. This ensures
that queued audio samples maintain valid configuration pointers if an
audio configuration update occurs before the key callback executes.

452730e5eea1ffbbc15ba1174f6d1fdbc1ff407e
parent
0fa769f074eed4672d038f5f0c9be0a5aa7bb02d [ diff ] 
updater: break the Configurator <-> RemoteLoggingDelegate cycle

`Configurator` owns the event logger, and the logger's delegate owned
the `Configurator`. Neither was ever destroyed, so the prefs and their
lock stayed alive until the process exited.

Move the delegate's main sequence work into a new
`RemoteLoggingUploader`, to which the delegate holds only a weak
pointer. `Configurator` owns the uploader and the logger through a new
`RemoteEventLogging`. Destroying the `Configurator` destroys the
uploader before the prefs; the logger may live on, but its uploads then
fail without touching the prefs.

parent
e49cea84da35f2471be82ef159c113d6d7b90a83 [ diff ] 
[e2e] Update control container top margin

Previously, ToolbarManager only updated the control container's top
margin inside onControlsOffsetChanged(). When top e2e is enabled,
non-animated top scalp height or visibility changes (e.g switching from
a custom NTP to a viewport-fit=cover tab) and animated toolbar position
transitions can occur without firing onControlsOffsetChanged(), leaving
the toolbar's top margin stale and overlapping the status bar

Extract updateControlContainerTopMargin() and invoke it on non-animated
onTopControlsHeightChanged() events (deferring animated height changes
to onControlsOffsetChanged() so the visible toolbar does not jump before
animation frames arrive) and on onControlsPositionChanged() when top e2e
is enabled. Also unregister any pending control container layout change
listener once its startup placeholder background is cleared or when
ToolbarManager is destroyed.

before: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/1sg279ctqpv2g
after: https://screenshot.corp.google.com/502hchtekava0

[TTC] Keep fullscreen inside the window in the fullscreen tool tests

ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow times out in
the first attempts of most builds on the Android Desktop bots, and so
does AndroidBookmarkBarActionAdapterBrowserTest.
OpenFolderNodes_NewWindow_OpensAllUrlsInNewWindow. The window they open
appears, but its creation never completes.

An earlier test in the same shard sets this off. On Android, a tab that
goes fullscreen also asks the OS to make its window fullscreen
(kDisplayEdgeToEdgeFullscreen), and on Android desktop the OS takes the
window out of the desktop for that and deactivates the desktop. The
tests of the enter_fullscreen and exit_fullscreen tools leave fullscreen
again within milliseconds. That can leave the window fullscreen for the
OS (crbug.com/585678116): FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase asks the OS to
undo the window's fullscreen only if the OS has confirmed it by then.
The test then ends with the desktop deactivated. In the failing build
that was examined, the test was
ActorWindowManagementToolBrowserTest.ExitFullscreen.

On a device with another app's window open on the desktop, which the
hardware bots have, the OS does not activate the desktop again after
that. A window opened from then on does not become the top resumed
activity, and ChromeAndroidTask completes a pending window only when it
does, so the callback of CreateBrowserWindow() never runs
(crbug.com/585677760). Rebooting the device, which the test runner does
before its last attempt, clears it.

These tests are about the tools, which put a tab into fullscreen and
take it out again, not about what the OS does with the window. Turn the
feature off in them on Android, so that fullscreen stays inside the
window and the desktop is left alone. The TTC test gets a fixture of its
own for this, which renames it to
ToolControllerFullscreenBrowserTest.EnterAndExitFullscreen.

This does not change the browser. The two bugs above remain.

    each of the three tests followed by
    ToolControllerWindowToolsBrowserTest.CreateAndCloseWindow

Bug: 585643190
Test: On the Android desktop emulator with another app's window open,
Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chromium.android:android-14-arm64-rel|android_browsertests

[TTC] Keep frames of other origins out of the get_pointer hit test

The server leaves the content of frames whose origin differs from the
page's out of what the model is given of the page. The hit test that
describes the element under the user's pointer searched such frames
too, so it could tell the model the label and text of something in
one.

DescribeNodeAtPoint() now describes nothing when the point is on a
frame of another origin, and leaves the text of such frames out of the
description of an element around one. A frame whose content was left
out of the page content, or whose origin is unknown, counts as one of
another origin.

Origins are compared as url::Origin::IsSameOriginWith() compares the
origins that they were serialized from: by scheme, host and port, and
by nonce if they are opaque. A unit test checks that against
url::Origin.

Design: go/ttc-get-pointer (CL C8 of the plan)

Bug: 571268337
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter=PointerHitTestTest.*

Extract field trial shared memory helpers and use flat_set

Downstream runtime field trial shared memory updates reuse trial
and feature serialization routines across startup population,
in-place appends, and segment compaction. In addition,
GetActiveTrialsOfParentProcess() returns a std::set that
allocates per-node heap entries for a small, read-mostly set of
inherited active trial names.

Extract shared memory pickle and entry allocation helpers in
FieldTrialList and FeatureList, factor startup allocator
population into PopulateSharedMemoryAllocatorWhileLocked(), and
change FieldTrialList::ActiveTrialNames to base::flat_set.

#runtime-mutability

Test: base_unittests --gtest_filter=FieldTrialListTest.*

parent
12075f098ba06a3edbeb082d133ba15f0980e832 [ diff ] 
Add generation_id metadata to FieldTrialAllocator

Child processes inherit initial field trials from shared memory
at startup and receive later runtime field trial updates over
Mojo IPC. Both paths need a monotonic sequence number to identify
which generation of variations state a child process holds.

Promote FieldTrialAllocator to a PersistentMemoryAllocator
subclass owning the shared memory mapping and store a 64-bit
atomic generation_id in a leading FieldTrialAllocatorMetadata
record. Keeping this metadata inside FieldTrialAllocator avoids
changing PersistentMemoryAllocator::SharedMetadata, preserving
persistent histogram (.pma) file compatibility across upgrades.
Enforce monotonic updates and expose acquire-release generation
accessors on FieldTrialAllocator and FieldTrialList.

#runtime-mutability

Test: base_unittests --gtest_filter=FieldTrialListTest.GenerationId
Bug: b:536851532, b:536852103

[TTC] Move get_pointer out of ToolController into GetPointerTool

ToolController should route tool calls, not implement tools, but
get_pointer's whole flow lived in it: four member functions and a
member for the capture in flight.

Move all of it into a class of its own, GetPointerTool, which the
controller creates if the tool's feature is enabled and calls Run() on.
Nothing about what the tool does changes.

The check that a page may still be captured once its URL has been
checked, which get_screenshot shares, becomes a free function next to
IsUrlSupportedForPageContext(). It takes the contents the session is
focused on rather than looking them up, so that it needs nothing from
ToolController.

get_pointer cannot be an actor tool yet: actor tools return a result
code and a message, not data or an image, which is also why
get_screenshot is not one. A TODO says to make it one once they can.

Design: go/ttc-get-pointer

    --gtest_filter=ToolControllerGetPointer*

Bug: 571268337
Test: browser_tests / android_browsertests

193ab3bad64c4361f48810f50616761072cb9490 [ diff ] 
[ContextualTasks] Keep ephemeral state in sync on pin and unpin

Under the ephemeral pin button refactor, the unpinned Contextual Tasks
button is a popped-out pinned toolbar action.

- When the action is pinned while popped out, do not clear the
  ephemeral state before PinnedToolbarActionsContainer has processed
  the pin change, so that the button is pinned in place instead of
  being queued for removal.
- When the action is unpinned, sync the ephemeral state explicitly so
  that stale state does not keep the unpinned button in the toolbar.

0c63504de7821b37d8f120dbc819798dfa158465
parent
261ac03b9f318280270e5fd4f09c3c492ebd044c [ diff ] 
Mojo TS Generator: Support webui_module_path = "REPO_PATHS"

Currently, TypeScript Mojo bindings require each mojom() target to
specify the served URL or relative path via webui_module_path so that
the generator can compute relative or URL-prefixed imports between
bindings. This computation logic is fragile, already broken for
some cases (e.g. extension clients, imports between cr_components),
and creates a hidden dependency on WebUI infrastructure details like
exact relative run time paths within the Mojo toolchain.

This CL adds support for setting webui_module_path = "REPO_PATHS" on a
mojom() target. When a module sets webui_module_path = "REPO_PATHS", the
generator emits repository-relative import paths of the form
"<path_to_mojo_target>/<module_name>.mojom-webui.js", leaving path
mapping to the consuming client (at build time or runtime). Any mojom
target generating TypeScript bindings that imports a "REPO_PATHS"
dependency must also set webui_module_path = "REPO_PATHS".

ee02edc943468cb06f7f3d00d5f8f3e21cdd4eff [ diff ] 
[drive_picker_host] Add kGlicDrivePicker flag and enable WebUI binders.

`chrome://drive-picker-host` and `chrome-untrusted://drive-picker-host`
were previously gated solely on
`omnibox::kComposeboxDriveContextMenuOption`, preventing Gemini in
Chrome from opening the Drive Picker independently of the omnibox
composebox feature.

Declare `features::kGlicDrivePicker` (disabled by default) and enable
`DrivePickerHostUIConfig`, `DrivePickerUntrustedHostUIConfig`, and their
desktop WebUI Mojo interface binders when either
`omnibox::kComposeboxDriveContextMenuOption` or
`features::kGlicDrivePicker` is enabled.

Design doc: https://artifacts.corp.google.com/WqlXtrwuU0?fullscreen=true&wide=true

3748265f0733ef8a3ad9f82c999f9c72b5c66742
parent
3563dbb4aa45333cd16cffc018e269c68d09fcd9 [ diff ] 
Measure indeterminate checkbox reset usage

In preparation for adding support for the defaultindeterminate attribute
for HTML checkboxes (which will allow for statically specifying the
indeterminateness of a checkbox), we want to measure how often an
indeterminate checkbox is reset. Today, the algorithm doesn't consider
indeterminateness, and its presence is not reset away. We would like
to measure how often this occurs, so that we can make an informed
decision for changing the reset behavior to include this in the future.

[media] Pause media on headphone unplug on Desktop Android

MediaSessionHelper skips the ACTION_AUDIO_BECOMING_NOISY pause on
Desktop Android, so audio keeps playing through the internal speakers
when a Bluetooth headset disconnects or a wired headset or HDMI audio
sink is unplugged.

Pause on all form factors by default instead, and replace the isDesktop
check with a PauseMediaOnHeadphoneUnplug kill switch (enabled by
default).

The new flag replaces NoPauseMediaOnHeadphoneUnplug so that the flag
isn't named negatively. Nothing reads the old flag, and its Finch study
ended in M153. Also remove its fieldtrial_testing_config.json entry,
which enabled it on Android bots.

This relies on the parent CL so that a later audio focus gain doesn't
resume the paused media.

  disconnected Bluetooth headphones while media played in several
  tabs. All tabs paused.

1c2f67dd9bb0b11f41f1566adcbb916685baff10

a535eb026b13c9a6d1eeb40b85d2852d4d971243 [ diff ] 
[media] Resume on audio focus gain only if focus loss paused media

Since crrev.com/c/8127655, Chrome on Android pauses media on headphone
unplug with SuspendType.SYSTEM so that the pause doesn't grant user
activation. Chrome keeps Android audio focus while paused, and
AudioFocusDelegateAndroid resumes any session suspended with kSystem
on AUDIOFOCUS_GAIN. So when another app takes transient focus (an
alarm, a call, a voice assistant) and releases it, the media starts
playing through the speaker with no user input.

Track in AudioFocusDelegateAndroid whether an audio focus loss
suspended the session, and only resume on a focus gain in that case.
The delegate clears that state once the session is no longer
suspended, however it was resumed or stopped. Guard the change with
kFocusGainResumesOnlyFocusLossPauses (enabled by default) as a kill
switch.

  headphones to pause media, then let a Clock timer ring and dismissed
  it. The media stayed paused.

Fixed: 585254746

e591968c14e0084b1e11e7172b8a316bddb1d722 [ diff ] 
Reset document observer on PiP close and guard session teardown

In PictureInPictureWindowManager::CloseWindowInternal(), reset
document_web_contents_observer_ alongside video_web_contents_observer_.
Previously, document_web_contents_observer_ remained active when a
Document Picture-in-Picture window was replaced or closed, causing
subsequent destruction of the former opener WebContents to improperly
clear the controller for any currently active Picture-in-Picture window.

Additionally, reset the Mojo receiver in PictureInPictureSession during
teardown and add early-return checks across session methods when
is_stopping_ is true. This ensures incoming calls after Stop() or
Disconnect() do not mutate player state or media session tracking during
session shutdown.

parent
b3faec3b38376310962aef71956acbe0dcf920dd [ diff ] 
Automated Clean-Up: Remove unused UKM <aggregation> for Security.SiteEngagement

An audit of UkmHistory_{1d,7d} over the past 12 months (b/584304583)
found 0 dashboard or F1 queries for these <aggregation> metrics (all
landed >300 days ago). Removing unused aggregations reduces our UKM
pipeline and storage costs. Note that UKM event-metrics collection and
non-aggregated tables are unaffected; only aggregated UkmHistory
entries are removed, and they will no longer be accessible on the UKM
dashboard. If needed in the future, this change can be reverted.

If this change looks good, please Code-Review+1. If your team
queries these in a private script, which our audit misses, please
comment to keep them; or we will land this cleanup after 5 business
days as UKM OWNERS.

[remoting host] Restore disconnect hotkey hint text to PrivacyOverlayWin

Adds a localized IDS_DISCONNECT_HOTKEY_HINT string ("Press
Ctrl+Alt+Esc to disconnect") and renders it beneath the client
sharing message on each PrivacyOverlayWin per-monitor backdrop:

- Defines IDS_DISCONNECT_HOTKEY_HINT in remoting_strings.grd,
  core_resource.h, and core.rc.jinja2 (with RC quote escaping).
- Factors shared ComputeBackdropContentLayout(), BackdropTextBlock,
  and GetBackdropDrawTextFlags() into host_window_win_util and uses
  them in both PaintBackdrop() and PositionDisconnectButton() so
  the Disconnect button and backdrop text never drift or overlap.
- Centralizes Ctrl+Alt+Esc registration in RegisterDisconnectHotKey()
  and UnregisterDisconnectHotKey() (shared with DisconnectWindowWin),
  rendering and announcing the hotkey hint only when registration
  succeeds and degrading gracefully on conflict or missing string.
- Adds unit tests covering ComputeBackdropContentLayout(),
  accessible newline separation, GDI rendering of the sharing and
  hint bands, button placement, and hermetic hotkey registration
  overrides.

[TabSearch] Revert chrome.histograms migration to restore metrics

Revert the Tab Search and tab_group_shared migration from
chrome.metricsPrivate to chrome.histograms from crrev.com/c/8748342
while keeping the WebUIContentsPreloadManager shutdown guard.

When WebiumMetricsMapping is enabled, SubprocessMetricsProvider filters
renderer histograms from all .top-chrome WebUIs through an allowlist,
dropping non-allowlisted chrome.histograms samples in Tab Search.
Recording via chrome.metricsPrivate executes in the browser process and
writes directly to StatisticsRecorder, bypassing
SubprocessMetricsProvider and MetricsNameMapper.

Add a browser test verifying Tab Search WebUI histograms are recorded
when WebiumMetricsMapping is enabled with only the default guardrail
rules.

dd5d8764d6bafc323f4d700c6573730f56cee86c [ diff ] 
Copy style attribute in FormatBlockCommand

When replacing a block element, FormatBlockCommand::FormatRange passed
the const AtomicString& from getAttribute(kStyleAttr) directly into
setAttribute() on the new block. If WillModifyAttribute() pauses on a
DevTools DOM breakpoint and script mutates the original element's
attributes, its AttributeVector can reallocate and leave that reference
dangling across AppendAttributeInternal(), so hold a local AtomicString
copy before calling setAttribute().

4016fc20938c028280d271c0901b7923d4151e32
parent
751a34ba0c295f6e84fa9e4e935a4457da77fc2b [ diff ] 
component_updater: remove the Android background task update scheduler

Since https://crrev.com/c/2891262 removed the
background-task-component-update flag in 2021, Chrome creates a
TimerUpdateScheduler on every platform, and nothing creates
BackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler. Its Java UpdateScheduler and UpdateTask
are still built into Chrome, and UpdateTask is still registered for
COMPONENT_UPDATE_JOB_ID. They could only run for a persisted job
scheduled by a Chrome that had the flag enabled, and such a job crashes
Chrome, because no native scheduler exists. UMA shows no such jobs:
Android.BackgroundTaskScheduler.TaskStarted records no component update
task starts in the 7 days ending on 2026-11-11.

Delete BackgroundTaskUpdateScheduler, UpdateScheduler, UpdateTask, and
their build targets, and stop mapping the job ID to a task. In release
builds, BackgroundTaskJobService cancels a leftover job because it
cannot create a task for it. Builds with Java asserts enabled assert in
ChromeBackgroundTaskFactory first, as they do for other retired task
IDs.

Rename the ID to DEPRECATED_COMPONENT_UPDATE_JOB_ID so it is not
reused, as was done for DEPRECATED_DOWNLOAD_RESUMPTION_JOB_ID, and
rename its UMA constant to match. Keep its UMA enum value, because
cancelling a leftover job records it in
Android.BackgroundTaskScheduler.TaskCanceled, and keep its histogram
variant, so that the Java and histograms.xml lists stay in sync. Also
remove the deleted directory from the Eclipse classpath.

https://crrev.com/c/8794631 removed a histogram that listed the deleted
OWNERS file as an owner. https://crrev.com/c/8797641 then removes the
on_stop callback from UpdateScheduler::Schedule(), which only the
deleted scheduler called.

20b9336e549089e6cfdc0ce0d41a1071b0b372f2
parent
70f7939f11970570fe3cfc86696c014316a1354e [ diff ] 
[Back-To-Ad] Send intervention report when back navigation skips an ad

When the back-to-ad intervention skips an ad entry during a back UI
navigation, send an intervention report to the pre-skip page, with the
main frame URL of the skipped ad entry (i.e., the entry the navigation
would otherwise have landed on).

The report is only sent if that main frame URL may be revealed to the
pre-skip document, i.e., if the skipped entry's main frame is
same-origin with the document, and its URL isn't hidden from the
document by referrer policy (as for `NavigationHistoryEntry.url`):
- This avoids leaking information about cross-origin entries.
- This is likely what the site can act upon anyway.

The report is sent when the navigation starts, rather than when it
commits, as the pre-skip document, which receives the report, is
unloaded by then. A report may thus be sent for a navigation that never
commits (e.g., one canceled by a beforeunload dialog); it reflects the
decision to skip the ad entry.

The report is also only sent when the back navigation starts from the
current page, which receives it (e.g., not when the user presses back
again before a previous back navigation finishes).

The reporting behavior is documented in
docs/history_manipulation_intervention.md.

73df6218de5ad70094beb296d8e3892fe6e11975 [ diff ] 
Simulate view focus in NavigationConsumesUserGesture_Fullscreen

Since SuppressFullscreenFromUnfocusedView became stable, the browser
rejects fullscreen requests from views without focus. browser_tests run
in parallel and can't rely on OS-level window activation, so the test's
first webkitRequestFullscreen() is rejected whenever its window lacks
focus. The following webkitExitFullscreen() then never fires a
fullscreen event and the test hangs until it times out.

On CQ this cost 301.57 in-shard re-run test-hours across 8,600
timeout-then-pass builds in the 7 days ending 2026-11-10, e.g.
https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665295745477916257.

Mark the view as focused with SetHasFocusForTesting(), as
https://crrev.com/c/8797552 did for the PDF fullscreen tests. The last
check, that fullscreen fails once a navigation has started, still holds
only because the navigation consumes the user activation.

UTR on linux-rel, browser_tests, with
--gtest_filter=NavigationConsumingTest.*:ChromeNavigationBrowserTest.*
--gtest_repeat=10:
- Before: 5/10 timed out, 5/10 passed.
  https://ci.chromium.org/ui/inv/u-mayurishinde-2026-11-13-23-24-00-1917678dc28571be
- After: 0/10 timed out, 10/10 passed in about 2 s each.
  https://ci.chromium.org/ui/inv/u-mayurishinde-2026-11-13-23-25-00-a49277e8bbd418e0
ChromeNavigationBrowserTest.NoopenerCorrectlyFocusesNewTab failed once
in each run and is unrelated to this change.

[omnibox][webui] Ignore extra fast enters while one waits for results

Pressing enter before the results for the latest input arrive waits for
them before navigating. If enter was pressed again meanwhile, the later
press replaced the earlier one.

With this CL, the earlier press keeps waiting and later ones are
ignored. This prepares for tab to wait for the latest results too.

tree
e26b4dc9da6ea3347219a10a2842808cce659116
parent
2ea142c3f41e31817b7d0302d96b312405c98b2f [ diff ] 
fuzzilli: Type pending associated remotes by the side that makes them

There are two bugs with how `_ILTypeName` determines interfaces
associated remote names:
- If JS never sends an associated remote of that interface, the type is
  not defined in the profile at all.
- Otherwise, the value is not usable: JS gets a
  `PendingAssociatedRemote` with no methods defined on it.

A `pending_associated_remote` that JS sends must still be typed
`PendingAssociatedRemote`, while the remote received from the browser
should be a `Remote` that has methods to call.

Pass `is_in_js` to `_ILTypeName()` so it knows which side makes the
value.

fuzzilli: Rename interface role sets in the Fuzzilli generator

`interface_remotes` and `interface_receivers` read like endpoint kinds,
which made conditions such as "not in interface_remotes but in
interface_pending_remotes" seem unclear / contradictory. Rename them to
`js_called_interfaces` and `js_implemented_interfaces` to reflect the
role JS plays for the interface, and clarify that the pending endpoint
sets are independent of these roles.

581f233a9464f8a0c59cf0673e842c07a48a46b5 [ diff ] 
fuzzilli: Support interfaces used as both remotes and receivers

An interface can be both a remote (bound from JS) and a receiver
(implemented in JS), such as FileSystemAccessTransferToken [1].
Previously such interfaces were only collected for one of the roles and
the generated profile did not model the other.

Collect both roles and skip the duplicate PendingReceiver/RemoteWrapper
definitions in that case.

[1] https://crsrc.org/c/third_party/blink/public/mojom/file_system_access/file_system_access_transfer_token.mojom;drc=1eeed2603fc3184c35d058ef7cd148a8c04d38d9

Make EventListenerMap::GetEventListenersByName return a span

Update EventListenerMap::GetEventListenersByName to return base::span
rather than a const reference to ListenerList. This allows returning an
empty span directly when no listeners exist, removing the static
NoDestructor empty list, and updates all callers to use the new return
type.

88ec5e93385a925d91cc8414e2c4a83667de5db7 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Stage Escape in the unbounded omnibox

Escape closed the dropdown in one step in the Full WebUI omnibox
hosted in the location bar. The unbounded window consumes the Escape
keydown and requests a dismissal; the page now cancels it and replays
the keydown on keyup (not while composing), so the first Escape reverts
to the default match, as in the popup widget. Copied from
webui_browser_searchbox.ts.

[webapps] Retire v6 web app DB upgrade flag [1/N]

`kWebAppUpgradeToDatabaseVersion6` has been enabled by default to
migrate `WebAppDatabase` to version 7. Removing the flag and its
fallback branch makes version 7 unconditional and eliminates version
skew ahead of migrating the database schema to version 8.

Ensure clean style and slot assignments before canvas transform update

This patch updates InvalidateForCanvasTransformChange to call
Document::UpdateStyleAndLayoutTree() before updating the PaintLayer
transform. Previously, calling PaintLayer::UpdateTransform() with dirty
style and slot assignments could trigger slot assignment
from ComputedStyle::ApplyTransform() and run a nested style update that
reset PaintLayer::transform_ while the outer call still held a
reference to it.

update_client: Let embedders limit the age of the ping freshness
📋 Metadata

9f5458841531559a72c761c60cdc29b0d7ea75ce
[ log ]
author

tree
01cb9f22e103782c60a8a05dfa9290163557dc43
parent
5076b71aa6b917f043f41ef61176d466da0f04d5 [ diff ] 
update_client: Let embedders limit the age of the ping freshness

update_client replaces the ping freshness (pf) only after a successful
update check. A client whose update checks keep failing to complete,
for example because it can't reach the update server or because the
process exits before the response arrives, sends the same pf in every
check for as long as the failures last. That makes the pf a stable
identifier of the client.

Give embedders what they need to limit the age of the pf that they
send:

* update_client::PersistedData records when it writes a pf, and gains
  GetPingFreshnessWriteTime() and RotatePingFreshness().
* The new GetPingFreshnessState() reports whether an app's pf is
  missing, fresh or expired, or whether its write time is unknown (for
  example, because an older version wrote the pf) or in the future (for
  example, because the clock was set back). A pf expires once it is 53
  hours (kPingFreshnessMaxAge) old.

What clients send doesn't change, since nothing rotates the pf yet. All
updaters now store the write time next to each pf, so most pf values
already have a known age by the time an updater starts to rotate them.
chrome/updater's PingPersistedData only sends pings, so its overrides
are NOTREACHED().

A follow-up CL, https://crrev.com/c/8809734, makes the activity
reporter replace its pf before an update check if the pf is expired, or
if its write time is unknown or in the future. Once the other updaters
are ready to do the same, update_client can do it before every update
check.

0ae80a06ad8971e3835f8be64090092149edf0c4
parent
51da6191bf8957b690b3a2c84d75a2f8b839be56 [ diff ] 
Skip excessive failures based on number of test on shards

Currently the broken_threshold is based on the total number of tests
in the test suite. We should do it based on the number of tests
that are ran on an individual shard. If  test suite has 1000 tests
and we shard it 10 ways, as written, this would require all 100 tests on a shard to timeout before we actually hit this threshold.

Currently we only skip tests when there are an excessive number of
timeouts: https://crrev.com/c/8797934/3/base/test/launcher/test_launcher.cc#1524

tree
690bab27e43831ca2a1a18ede713e21ae45bcded
parent
31fd02747d6f15d322bcbdbf50348b5d3d8a8de3 [ diff ] 
[Glic] Split BuildTabContextResult and add post-fetch tab check

PageContextFetcher observes WebContentsDestroyed and PrimaryPageChanged
during a fetch, but is destroyed once HandleFetchPageResult() runs.
Previously, HandleFetchPageResult() built TabContextResult
synchronously, so no post-fetch tab check was needed.

Follow-up DLP changes insert an async FILE_ATTACHED PDF scan before
building TabContextResult and re-check DataControlsRules against the
tab's WebContents afterward. During that window, PageContextFetcher no
longer observes the tab, which can close or navigate (including into
BackForwardCache).

Extract BuildTabContextResult() and gate it on CheckTabStillShareable()
(using a WeakPtr to the initial primary Page) so the tab and primary
page are verified before building TabContextResult. Also forward
fetch_result.error() directly in HandleFetchPageResult() instead of
overwriting error_code with kUnknown.

c9362957cd0c062988b269f6a533f444da0dd03a
parent
4b6765a3fe2e82458d1811bca527fa147ca4b8cf [ diff ] 
Update omnibox rich images without rebuilding the popup

Rich suggestion images can arrive synchronously from the bitmap cache
after a result change. Notifying OnContentsChanged for each bitmap
repeats match, accessibility and layout updates for every popup row.

Add a row-scoped observer notification with a full-update default for
existing observers. The Views popup updates only the affected rich image
when eligible, retaining full refreshes for closed or ineligible popups,
stale indices, missing rows and suggestions that render images as icons.

Restore square image sizing when replacing a rectangular image, without
relying on a preceding SetMatch call. Add observer compatibility and
browser coverage, including real synchronous cache hits, row isolation,
image sizing and fallback paths.

The cache-hit test verifies one full refresh instead of three when two
cached images arrive during a result update.

tree
0da5f43baf9534a76c9984760e3dc8d69df837b6
parent
d72a35a9ce7d99322567eadb9291962dbe7f46b2 [ diff ] 
[Mac] Disallow external drag for empty DropData and sanitize EndDrag operation

When dragging an internal UI element with an empty DropData payload (such
as reordering profile cards in the Profile Picker, which clears
dataTransfer.items) onto the macOS Dock Trash:

1. -[WebContentsViewCocoa draggingSession:sourceOperationMaskForDraggingContext:]
   previously returned _dragOperation even for
   NSDraggingContextOutsideApplication when DropData had no external
   payload. Return NSDragOperationNone outside the application when
   DropData contains no URLs, files, downloads, HTML, plain text, or
   custom data so external targets like the Dock Trash do not accept
   empty-payload drags.

2. -[WebContentsViewCocoa draggingSession:endedAtPoint:operation:]
   previously passed the raw AppKit NSDragOperation value directly to
   _host->EndDrag(), which casts it to ui::mojom::DragOperation and
   sends it over Mojo in FrameWidget::DragSourceEndedAt. When macOS
   ended a drag on the Dock Trash with NSDragOperationDelete (32), it
   failed Mojo enum validation (ui::mojom::DragOperation only defines
   kNone, kCopy, kLink, kMove), dropping the DragSourceEndedAt message
   so the DOM 'dragend' event never fired in JS and the dragged card
   remained hidden. Convert the operation via
   ui::DragDropTypes::NSDragOperationToDragOperation() before calling
   EndDrag().

TAG=agy
CONV=d54b0b4f-0ec6-4f79-883f-91081dedf382

Revert "[Memory] Re-enable `DumpsAreAddedToTraceWhenRequested` test"

This reverts https://crrev.com/c/8691455.

After re-enabling
`CoordinatorImplTest.DumpsAreAddedToTraceWhenRequested`, the test failed
across platforms at `74.72%` (`4,543` flaky/failed runs out of `6,080`),
including `95.4%` (`311/326`) on `Linux Tests`, `97.2%` (`245/252`) on
`linux-perfetto-rel`, `97.5%` (`230/236`) on `mac-rel-cft`, and `97.7%`
(`210/215`) on `mac26-arm64-rel-tests` due to unexpected
`OnCall(kInvalidTracingState, 1)` mock calls and aborts.

Data: flaky plus failed verdicts out of all CI verdicts from
2026-11-03T00:43:19Z (landing of https://crrev.com/c/8691455) to
2026-11-07T00:00:00Z in the `chromium` project, summed over the test ID
returned by `luci.analysis.v1.TestHistory.QueryTests` and grouped by the
`builder` of each variant (from `QueryVariants`). Command:

    p=chromium; q='CoordinatorImplTest#'
    q+='DumpsAreAddedToTraceWhenRequested'
    jq -nc --arg p "$p" --arg q "$q" \
      '{project:$p,testIdSubstring:$q}' | \
      prpc call analysis.api.luci.app \
      luci.analysis.v1.TestHistory.QueryTests | \
      jq -r '.testIds[]?' | while IFS= read -r tid; do
      echo "=== $tid ==="
      { jq -nc --arg p "$p" --arg t "$tid" \
          '{project:$p,testId:$t,subRealm:"ci",pageSize:1000}' | \
          prpc call analysis.api.luci.app \
          luci.analysis.v1.TestHistory.QueryVariants
        jq -nc --arg p "$p" --arg t "$tid" \
          '{project:$p,testId:$t,pageSize:1000,predicate:{
            subRealm:"ci",partitionTimeRange:{
              earliest:"2026-11-03T00:43:19Z",
              latest:"2026-11-07T00:00:00Z"}}}' | \
          prpc call analysis.api.luci.app \
          luci.analysis.v1.TestHistory.QueryStats
      } | jq -rs '
        ([.[0].variants[]?|{(.variantHash):.variant.def.builder}]
         |add//{}) as $m | [.[1].groups[]? | {
          b:($m[.variantHash]//"unknown"),
          bad:((.verdictCounts.failed//0)+(.verdictCounts.flaky//0)),
          tot:([.verdictCounts[]?]|add//0)}]
        | "TOTAL\t\(([.[].bad]|add))/\(([.[].tot]|add))",
          (group_by(.b)[] | select(([.[].bad]|add) > 0) |
           "\(([.[].bad]|add))/\(([.[].tot]|add))\t\(.[0].b)")'
    done

Test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium/%3A%2F%2Fservices%5C%3Aservices_unittests%21gtest%3A%3ACoordinatorImplTest%23DumpsAreAddedToTraceWhenRequested

Part of http://go/arthurmilchior-fix-all-test-skill-chrome for AI week.

[Omnibox] Clip the selection listbox in place in the unbounded omnibox

Arrowing in the unbounded location bar omnibox un-hides
the unbounded surface past the 8192px texture limit and blanks the
dropdown. Add an opt-in clip-selection-listbox attribute on
cr-searchbox-input to clip it in place instead, and set it only from
the unbounded location bar omnibox so other embedders are unchanged.

[Actor] Post unpinned opt-in notification when a task holds FGS

When an opt-in request arrives while a task holds
ActorForegroundService, or when a task starts while
OPT_IN_NOTIFICATION_ID is pinned, ActorForegroundServiceManager
previously cancelled the opt-in notification and failed immediately,
even though the task already keeps the foreground service alive.

- Update ActorNotificationFactory to support building unpinned and
  silent opt-in notifications via an isPinned parameter, and wire
  the delete intent to trigger cancellation when dismissed.
- Encapsulate unpinned opt-in notification management inside
  ActorNotificationService so notification tag handling remains
  within the notifications package.
- In ActorForegroundServiceManager, post unpinned opt-in notifications
  when another notification holds the service, re-pin
  OPT_IN_NOTIFICATION_ID silently once the task finishes, and handle
  opt-in pin side-effects directly inside
  startOrUpdateForegroundService.
- Add unit tests across active and pending task transitions.

b51611ff717095d88f7962bb2c5ec79f9efa1de5
parent
02233da21b9662ca76a545d017660ac70a4c5eb3 [ diff ] 
Skip in-app update discovery snackbar when other UI is showing

The discovery snackbar is shown once per launch, shortly after
startup, where it can compete with a startup promo, a modal dialog
or a bottom sheet. Next to that UI it is easy to miss.

Skip the discovery snackbar when a startup promo was shown in this
session, or when a modal dialog or a bottom sheet (including a
peeking one) is on screen. A skip records no impression and no
cooldown, so the snackbar is offered again on the next cold start.
The reason goes into three new Skipped buckets of
Android.InAppUpdate.DiscoverySnackbar.Event.

The promo check is best effort. The startup promo flow is not
ordered before the update check, so in rare cases a promo can still
appear after the snackbar is shown.

Another snackbar on screen is not a reason to skip, because
SnackbarManager already queues the discovery snackbar behind it.

The restart snackbar is unchanged. It shows soon after the user
accepts the Play sheet, and background install may replace it.

Keep shared image data through cache writes

tree
a382d7fad46bb6fe29e81199298b8ddafa9f50c9
parent
60072aecd6bb8449b54247ede0339fc98c8d87ab [ diff ] 
Keep shared image data through cache writes

Downloaded image bytes already live in a shared buffer, but saving them
in the disk cache copies them into an owning string. PNG and JPEG
transcoding also copies encoder output into strings before saving it.
Pass shared buffers through the cache write path and move encoder output
into them to avoid these redundant allocations and full-buffer copies.

Queued initialization and disk writes retain the buffers until they are
consumed. The cache read path and on-disk format remain unchanged.
